# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title joseph Model

class DirectoryFilter {
    ignoreDirs : List<String>
    isIgnored(path : String) : boolean
    isIgnoredPath(path : Path) : boolean
}
class DirectoryToMarkdown {
    directoryFilter : DirectoryFilter
    fileContentReader : FileContentReader
    markdownWriter : MarkdownWriter
    convert(options : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions) : MarkdownConversionResult
    createDefaultIgnoreDirs() : List<String>
    createDefaultOptions() : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
    createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath : Path, extension : String, content : String) : MarkdownFileEntry
    createOptions(directoryPath : Path, outputFile : Path, fileTypes : List<String>, ignoreDirs : List<String>, recursive : boolean, maxFileSizeMb : int) : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
    directoryToMarkdown(directoryPath : Path, outputFile : Path, fileTypes : List<String>, ignoreDirs : List<String>, recursive : boolean, maxFileSizeMb : int) : int
    getExtension(path : Path) : String
    isIgnored(path : String, ignoreDirs : List<String>) : boolean
    readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String) : String
    readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : String
    readFileSafelyfromPath(filePath : Path) : String
    readFileSafelyfromPathSizeMb(filePath : Path, maxSizeMb : int) : String
    shouldIncludeExtension(extension : String, fileTypes : List<String>) : boolean
    walkDirectory(currentDir : Path, md : BufferedWriter, optons : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, baseDir : Path, result : MarkdownConversionResult) : void
}
class DirectoryToMarkdownOptions {
    directoryPath : String
    fileTypes : List<String>
    ignoreDirs : List<String>
    maxFileSizeMb : int
    outputFile : String
    recursive : boolean
}
class FileContentReader {
    readFileSafely(filePath : Path, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
    readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
}
class FileReadResult {
    content : String
    readable : boolean
    skippedReason : SkipReason
}
class MarkdownConversionResult {
    processedFileCount : int
    processedFiles : List<MarkdownFileEntry>
    addProcessedFile(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : void
}
class MarkdownFileEntry {
    content : String
    extension : String
    language : String
    relativePath : String
}
class MarkdownWriter {
    formatEntry(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : String
}
enum SkipReason {
    NONE
    TOO_LARGE
    UNREADABLE
}

DirectoryToMarkdown --> DirectoryFilter : directoryFilter
DirectoryToMarkdown --> FileContentReader : fileContentReader
DirectoryToMarkdown --> MarkdownWriter : markdownWriter
MarkdownConversionResult *-- "*" MarkdownFileEntry : processedFiles
@enduml
```

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Operation Specifications
FileContentReader::readFileSafely
Summary: Safely reads a file as UTF-8 text, enforcing a maximum size, and returns a result with a skip reason.
Algorithm:
  1. If filePath is null or does not exist, return FileReadResult(null, false, SkipReason.UNREADABLE).
  2. If filePath is not a regular file, return FileReadResult(null, false, SkipReason.UNREADABLE).
  3. If maxSizeMb <= 0, set maxSizeMb = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_SIZE_MB (10).
  4. Compute maxBytes = maxSizeMb * 1024L * 1024L.
  5. If Files.size(filePath) > maxBytes, return FileReadResult(null, false, SkipReason.TOO_LARGE).
  6. Try to read all bytes from the file and decode them as UTF-8.
  7. On success, return FileReadResult(content, true, SkipReason.NONE).
  8. On IOException, SecurityException, or decoding failure, return FileReadResult(null, false, SkipReason.UNREADABLE).
Related Features: None
Related Classes: FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input: filePath: Path - file to read; maxSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes; if <=0, the default 10 is used.
Output: FileReadResult - content (String or null), readable (boolean), skippedReason (SkipReason).
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null; maxSizeMb is an integer value representing megabytes.
Post-condition: If readable is true, content contains the full UTF-8 text and skippedReason is NONE; if readable is false, content is null and skippedReason is TOO_LARGE or UNREADABLE.

FileContentReader::readFileSafelyFromString
Summary: Converts a string file path to a Path and delegates to readFileSafely.
Algorithm:
  1. Return readFileSafely(Paths.get(filePath), maxSizeMb).
Related Features: readFileSafely()
Related Classes: FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input: filePath: String - path to the file; maxSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes.
Output: FileReadResult - content, readable, and skippedReason as produced by readFileSafely.
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null and can be converted to a Path; maxSizeMb is an integer value.
Post-condition: No state change; result reflects the file on disk.

```plantuml
    readFileSafely(filePath : Path, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
    readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
}
class FileReadResult {
    content : String
    readable : boolean
    skippedReason : SkipReason
}
class MarkdownConversionResult {
    processedFileCount : int
    processedFiles : List<MarkdownFileEntry>
    addProcessedFile(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : void
}
class MarkdownFileEntry {
    content : String
    extension : String
    language : String
    relativePath : String
}
class MarkdownWriter {
    formatEntry(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : String
}
enum SkipReason {
    NONE
    TOO_LARGE
    UNREADABLE
}

DirectoryToMarkdown --> DirectoryFilter : directoryFilter
DirectoryToMarkdown --> FileContentReader : fileContentReader
DirectoryToMarkdown --> MarkdownWriter : markdownWriter
MarkdownConversionResult *-- "*" MarkdownFileEntry : processedFiles
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
FileContentReader::readFileSafely
Summary: Safely reads a file as UTF-8 text, enforcing a maximum size, and returns a result with a skip reason.
Algorithm:
  1. If filePath is null or does not exist, return FileReadResult(null, false, SkipReason.UNREADABLE).
  2. If filePath is not a regular file, return FileReadResult(null, false, SkipReason.UNREADABLE).
  3. If maxSizeMb <= 0, set maxSizeMb = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_SIZE_MB (10).
  4. Compute maxBytes = maxSizeMb * 1024L * 1024L.
  5. If Files.size(filePath) > maxBytes, return FileReadResult(null, false, SkipReason.TOO_LARGE).
  6. Try to read all bytes from the file and decode them as UTF-8.
  7. On success, return FileReadResult(content, true, SkipReason.NONE).
  8. On IOException, SecurityException, or decoding failure, return FileReadResult(null, false, SkipReason.UNREADABLE).
Related Features: None
Related Classes: FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input: filePath: Path - file to read; maxSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes; if <=0, the default 10 is used.
Output: FileReadResult - content (String or null), readable (boolean), skippedReason (SkipReason).
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null; maxSizeMb is an integer value representing megabytes.
Post-condition: If readable is true, content contains the full UTF-8 text and skippedReason is NONE; if readable is false, content is null and skippedReason is TOO_LARGE or UNREADABLE.

FileContentReader::readFileSafelyFromString
Summary: Converts a string file path to a Path and delegates to readFileSafely.
Algorithm:
  1. Return readFileSafely(Paths.get(filePath), maxSizeMb).
Related Features: readFileSafely()
Related Classes: FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input: filePath: String - path to the file; maxSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes.
Output: FileReadResult - content, readable, and skippedReason as produced by readFileSafely.
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null and can be converted to a Path; maxSizeMb is an integer value.
Post-condition: No state change; result reflects the file on disk.

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb
Summary: Reads a file from a string path, enforces a maximum size, and returns its content if readable.
Algorithm:
  1. Convert filePath to Path via Paths.get(filePath).
  2. Call fileContentReader.readFileSafely(path, maxSizeMb).
  3. If result.readable is true, return result.content; otherwise return null.
Related Features: fileContentReader
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult
Input: filePath: String - path to the file; maxSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes.
Output: String - file content as UTF-8 text, or null if the file is too large or unreadable.
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null and can be converted to a Path; maxSizeMb > 0.
Post-condition: No state change; if the file is readable, the returned string matches the file content; otherwise null is returned.

DirectoryToMarkdown::directoryToMarkdown
Summary: Convenience entry point that converts a directory to Markdown and returns the number of processed files.
Algorithm:
  1. Build options using createOptions(directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, maxFileSizeMb).
  2. Call convert(options).
  3. Return result.processedFileCount.
Related Features: createOptions(), convert()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult
Input: directoryPath: Path - root directory; outputFile: Path - target Markdown file; fileTypes: List<String> - allowed extensions; ignoreDirs: List<String> - ignored directory names; recursive: boolean - whether to scan subdirectories; maxFileSizeMb: int - maximum file size in MB.
Output: int - number of files successfully processed and added to the Markdown document.
Pre-condition: directoryPath exists and is a directory; outputFile path is writable; fileTypes and ignoreDirs are non-null.
Post-condition: The Markdown output file is written; the returned count equals the number of MarkdownFileEntry objects generated.

DirectoryToMarkdown::walkDirectory
Summary: Recursively walks a directory in stable order and writes Markdown sections for eligible files.
Algorithm:
  1. List all entries in currentDir and sort them by filename using natural lexicographic order.
  2. For each entry:
     a. Compute relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry).
     b. If directoryFilter.isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) or directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(relativePath), skip this entry entirely.
     c. If entry is a directory:
         - If optons.recursive is true, call walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result).
         - Otherwise do nothing.
     d. Else if entry is a regular file:
         - extension = getExtension(entry).
         - If shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.fileTypes) is false, skip the file.
         - Read the file with fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb).
         - If result.readable is false, skip the file. The FileReadResult.skippedReason distinguishes TOO_LARGE from UNREADABLE.
         - Otherwise create markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, result.content).
         - Write markdownWriter.formatEntry(markdownEntry) to md, then write a blank line separator.
         - Add markdownEntry to result via result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry).
  3. Return void.
Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, getExtension(), shouldIncludeExtension(), createMarkdownFileEntry(), result.addProcessedFile()
Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult, MarkdownWriter, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input: currentDir: Path - directory currently being visited; md: BufferedWriter - output writer; optons: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - configuration settings; baseDir: Path - root directory used to compute relative paths; result: MarkdownConversionResult - accumulator for processed files.
Output: void - the operation appends formatted Markdown to md and updates result.
Pre-condition: currentDir exists and is a directory; md is open; optons and result are non-null; baseDir is the configured root directory.
Post-condition: All eligible files under currentDir, subject to recursion and filters, have been written in deterministic order; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size().

DirectoryToMarkdown::createDefaultOptions
Summary: Creates a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions object populated with default values for optional settings.
Algorithm:
  1. Create a new DirectoryToMarkdownOptions.
  2. Set directoryPath = ""; outputFile = ""; fileTypes = empty List<String>; ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs() (node_modules, __pycache__, .git, .vscode, .idea); recursive = true; maxFileSizeMb = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_SIZE_MB (10).
  3. Return the options object.
Related Features: createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input: None.
Output: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - a non-null object with default optional settings; directoryPath and outputFile are empty and must be set by the caller before conversion.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: A new options object is returned with default ignoreDirs, recursive=true, maxFileSizeMb=10, and an empty fileTypes list.

DirectoryToMarkdown::getExtension
Summary: Returns the lower-case extension of a file path, including the leading dot.
Algorithm:
  1. Get fileName = path.getFileName().toString(); if fileName is null, return "".
  2. Find the last dot index in fileName.
  3. If no dot exists, return "".
  4. Return fileName.substring(dotIndex).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: path: Path - a file path or file name; non-null.
Output: String - extension including the leading dot, e.g. ".java"; empty string if the file name has no dot.
Pre-condition: path is non-null.
Post-condition: No state change; returned extension is lower-case.

DirectoryToMarkdown::isIgnored
Summary: Tests whether a path contains any ignored directory component.
Algorithm:
  1. If path is null or ignoreDirs is null/empty, return false.
  2. Normalize path by replacing '\' with '/'.
  3. Split normalized path on '/'.
  4. For each path component, if ignoreDirs.contains(component), return true.
  5. Return false.
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: path: String - a relative path string; ignoreDirs: List<String> - directory names to ignore.
Output: boolean - true if any path component exactly matches an ignored directory name.
Pre-condition: path and ignoreDirs are non-null.
Post-condition: No state change.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createMarkdownFileEntry
Summary: Builds a MarkdownFileEntry from a relative path, extension, and file content.
Algorithm:
  1. Convert relativePath.toString() to a normalized relative string, replacing '\' with '/'.
  2. Normalize extension to lower-case. If extension is null or empty, set language = ""; otherwise set language = extension without the leading dot.
  3. Return new MarkdownFileEntry(content, extension, language, relativePathString).
Related Features: None
Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
Input: relativePath: Path - non-null, relative to the base directory; extension: String - file extension including the leading dot, e.g. ".java", possibly empty; content: String - UTF-8 text content.
Output: MarkdownFileEntry - fields content, extension, language, and relativePath are populated.
Pre-condition: relativePath and content are non-null; extension, if non-empty, starts with '.' and contains no path separators.
Post-condition: entry.language equals extension without the leading dot; entry.relativePath is normalized with forward slashes.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createOptions
Summary: Converts and normalizes primitive option inputs into a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions object.
Algorithm:
  1. If fileTypes == null, set fileTypes = emptyList.
  2. If ignoreDirs == null, set ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs(); if ignoreDirs is empty, leave it empty.
  3. If maxFileSizeMb <= 0, set maxFileSizeMb = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_SIZE_MB (10).
  4. Create DirectoryToMarkdownOptions with directoryPath.toString(), outputFile.toString(), fileTypes, ignoreDirs, maxFileSizeMb, and recursive.
  5. Return the options object.
Related Features: createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input: directoryPath: Path - root directory to scan; outputFile: Path - target Markdown file; fileTypes: List<String> - allowed lower-case extensions including leading dot; ignoreDirs: List<String> - ignored directory names; recursive: boolean - true to traverse subdirectories; maxFileSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes.
Output: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - non-null object carrying normalized settings.
Pre-condition: directoryPath and outputFile are non-null; directoryPath is expected to be a directory.
Post-condition: Returns an options object; null lists become empty/default lists, and non-positive maxFileSizeMb is replaced by 10.

DirectoryToMarkdown::convert
Summary: Orchestrates conversion of a configured directory into a Markdown document and returns the conversion result.
Algorithm:
  1. Validate that options is non-null.
  2. Create MarkdownConversionResult result = new MarkdownConversionResult().
  3. Create parent directories for options.outputFile if they do not exist.
  4. Open a BufferedWriter with UTF-8 encoding for options.outputFile.
  5. Call walkDirectory(Paths.get(options.directoryPath), writer, options, Paths.get(options.directoryPath), result).
  6. Close the writer in a try-with-resources block.
  7. Return result.
Related Features: walkDirectory()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult
Input: options: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - contains directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, and maxFileSizeMb.
Output: MarkdownConversionResult - contains processedFileCount and processedFiles; the output Markdown file is also written.
Pre-condition: options is non-null; options.directoryPath exists and is a readable directory; options.outputFile is writable or its parent directory can be created; fileTypes and ignoreDirs are non-null; maxFileSizeMb > 0.
Post-condition: The output file contains a Markdown section for each processed file; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size(); the file system reflects the written Markdown document.

DirectoryToMarkdown::shouldIncludeExtension
Summary: Decides whether a file extension passes the configured file type filter.
Algorithm:
  1. If fileTypes is null or empty, return true.
  2. Normalize extension to lower-case.
  3. For each type in fileTypes, if type is non-null and type.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).equals(extension), return true.
  4. Return false.
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: extension: String - file extension including the leading dot, e.g. ".java"; fileTypes: List<String> - allowed extensions including leading dot.
Output: boolean - true if no filter is configured or extension is included in fileTypes; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: extension is non-null; fileTypes is non-null or treated as empty.
Post-condition: No state change.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyfromPathSizeMb
Summary: Reads a file from a Path, enforces a maximum size, and returns its content if readable.
Algorithm:
  1. Call fileContentReader.readFileSafely(filePath, maxSizeMb).
  2. If result.readable is true, return result.content; otherwise return null.
Related Features: fileContentReader
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult
Input: filePath: Path - path to the file; maxSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes.
Output: String - file content as UTF-8 text, or null if the file is too large or unreadable.
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null; maxSizeMb > 0.
Post-condition: No state change; if readable, content is returned; otherwise null.

```plantuml
    UNREADABLE
}

DirectoryToMarkdown --> DirectoryFilter : directoryFilter
DirectoryToMarkdown --> FileContentReader : fileContentReader
DirectoryToMarkdown --> MarkdownWriter : markdownWriter
MarkdownConversionResult *-- "*" MarkdownFileEntry : processedFiles
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb
Summary: Reads a file from a string path, enforces a maximum size, and returns its content if readable.
Algorithm:
  1. Convert filePath to Path via Paths.get(filePath).
  2. Call fileContentReader.readFileSafely(path, maxSizeMb).
  3. If result.readable is true, return result.content; otherwise return null.
Related Features: fileContentReader
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult
Input: filePath: String - path to the file; maxSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes.
Output: String - file content as UTF-8 text, or null if the file is too large or unreadable.
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null and can be converted to a Path; maxSizeMb > 0.
Post-condition: No state change; if the file is readable, the returned string matches the file content; otherwise null is returned.

DirectoryToMarkdown::directoryToMarkdown
Summary: Convenience entry point that converts a directory to Markdown and returns the number of processed files.
Algorithm:
  1. Build options using createOptions(directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, maxFileSizeMb).
  2. Call convert(options).
  3. Return result.processedFileCount.
Related Features: createOptions(), convert()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult
Input: directoryPath: Path - root directory; outputFile: Path - target Markdown file; fileTypes: List<String> - allowed extensions; ignoreDirs: List<String> - ignored directory names; recursive: boolean - whether to scan subdirectories; maxFileSizeMb: int - maximum file size in MB.
Output: int - number of files successfully processed and added to the Markdown document.
Pre-condition: directoryPath exists and is a directory; outputFile path is writable; fileTypes and ignoreDirs are non-null.
Post-condition: The Markdown output file is written; the returned count equals the number of MarkdownFileEntry objects generated.

DirectoryToMarkdown::walkDirectory
Summary: Recursively walks a directory in stable order and writes Markdown sections for eligible files.
Algorithm:
  1. List all entries in currentDir and sort them by filename using natural lexicographic order.
  2. For each entry:
     a. Compute relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry).
     b. If directoryFilter.isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) or directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(relativePath), skip this entry entirely.
     c. If entry is a directory:
         - If optons.recursive is true, call walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result).
         - Otherwise do nothing.
     d. Else if entry is a regular file:
         - extension = getExtension(entry).
         - If shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.fileTypes) is false, skip the file.
         - Read the file with fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb).
         - If result.readable is false, skip the file. The FileReadResult.skippedReason distinguishes TOO_LARGE from UNREADABLE.
         - Otherwise create markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, result.content).
         - Write markdownWriter.formatEntry(markdownEntry) to md, then write a blank line separator.
         - Add markdownEntry to result via result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry).
  3. Return void.
Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, getExtension(), shouldIncludeExtension(), createMarkdownFileEntry(), result.addProcessedFile()
Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult, MarkdownWriter, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input: currentDir: Path - directory currently being visited; md: BufferedWriter - output writer; optons: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - configuration settings; baseDir: Path - root directory used to compute relative paths; result: MarkdownConversionResult - accumulator for processed files.
Output: void - the operation appends formatted Markdown to md and updates result.
Pre-condition: currentDir exists and is a directory; md is open; optons and result are non-null; baseDir is the configured root directory.
Post-condition: All eligible files under currentDir, subject to recursion and filters, have been written in deterministic order; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size().

DirectoryToMarkdown::createDefaultOptions
Summary: Creates a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions object populated with default values for optional settings.
Algorithm:
  1. Create a new DirectoryToMarkdownOptions.
  2. Set directoryPath = ""; outputFile = ""; fileTypes = empty List<String>; ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs() (node_modules, __pycache__, .git, .vscode, .idea); recursive = true; maxFileSizeMb = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_SIZE_MB (10).
  3. Return the options object.
Related Features: createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input: None.
Output: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - a non-null object with default optional settings; directoryPath and outputFile are empty and must be set by the caller before conversion.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: A new options object is returned with default ignoreDirs, recursive=true, maxFileSizeMb=10, and an empty fileTypes list.

DirectoryToMarkdown::getExtension
Summary: Returns the lower-case extension of a file path, including the leading dot.
Algorithm:
  1. Get fileName = path.getFileName().toString(); if fileName is null, return "".
  2. Find the last dot index in fileName.
  3. If no dot exists, return "".
  4. Return fileName.substring(dotIndex).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: path: Path - a file path or file name; non-null.
Output: String - extension including the leading dot, e.g. ".java"; empty string if the file name has no dot.
Pre-condition: path is non-null.
Post-condition: No state change; returned extension is lower-case.

DirectoryToMarkdown::isIgnored
Summary: Tests whether a path contains any ignored directory component.
Algorithm:
  1. If path is null or ignoreDirs is null/empty, return false.
  2. Normalize path by replacing '\' with '/'.
  3. Split normalized path on '/'.
  4. For each path component, if ignoreDirs.contains(component), return true.
  5. Return false.
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: path: String - a relative path string; ignoreDirs: List<String> - directory names to ignore.
Output: boolean - true if any path component exactly matches an ignored directory name.
Pre-condition: path and ignoreDirs are non-null.
Post-condition: No state change.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createMarkdownFileEntry
Summary: Builds a MarkdownFileEntry from a relative path, extension, and file content.
Algorithm:
  1. Convert relativePath.toString() to a normalized relative string, replacing '\' with '/'.
  2. Normalize extension to lower-case. If extension is null or empty, set language = ""; otherwise set language = extension without the leading dot.
  3. Return new MarkdownFileEntry(content, extension, language, relativePathString).
Related Features: None
Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
Input: relativePath: Path - non-null, relative to the base directory; extension: String - file extension including the leading dot, e.g. ".java", possibly empty; content: String - UTF-8 text content.
Output: MarkdownFileEntry - fields content, extension, language, and relativePath are populated.
Pre-condition: relativePath and content are non-null; extension, if non-empty, starts with '.' and contains no path separators.
Post-condition: entry.language equals extension without the leading dot; entry.relativePath is normalized with forward slashes.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createOptions
Summary: Converts and normalizes primitive option inputs into a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions object.
Algorithm:
  1. If fileTypes == null, set fileTypes = emptyList.
  2. If ignoreDirs == null, set ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs(); if ignoreDirs is empty, leave it empty.
  3. If maxFileSizeMb <= 0, set maxFileSizeMb = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_SIZE_MB (10).
  4. Create DirectoryToMarkdownOptions with directoryPath.toString(), outputFile.toString(), fileTypes, ignoreDirs, maxFileSizeMb, and recursive.
  5. Return the options object.
Related Features: createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input: directoryPath: Path - root directory to scan; outputFile: Path - target Markdown file; fileTypes: List<String> - allowed lower-case extensions including leading dot; ignoreDirs: List<String> - ignored directory names; recursive: boolean - true to traverse subdirectories; maxFileSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes.
Output: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - non-null object carrying normalized settings.
Pre-condition: directoryPath and outputFile are non-null; directoryPath is expected to be a directory.
Post-condition: Returns an options object; null lists become empty/default lists, and non-positive maxFileSizeMb is replaced by 10.

DirectoryToMarkdown::convert
Summary: Orchestrates conversion of a configured directory into a Markdown document and returns the conversion result.
Algorithm:
  1. Validate that options is non-null.
  2. Create MarkdownConversionResult result = new MarkdownConversionResult().
  3. Create parent directories for options.outputFile if they do not exist.
  4. Open a BufferedWriter with UTF-8 encoding for options.outputFile.
  5. Call walkDirectory(Paths.get(options.directoryPath), writer, options, Paths.get(options.directoryPath), result).
  6. Close the writer in a try-with-resources block.
  7. Return result.
Related Features: walkDirectory()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult
Input: options: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - contains directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, and maxFileSizeMb.
Output: MarkdownConversionResult - contains processedFileCount and processedFiles; the output Markdown file is also written.
Pre-condition: options is non-null; options.directoryPath exists and is a readable directory; options.outputFile is writable or its parent directory can be created; fileTypes and ignoreDirs are non-null; maxFileSizeMb > 0.
Post-condition: The output file contains a Markdown section for each processed file; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size(); the file system reflects the written Markdown document.

DirectoryToMarkdown::shouldIncludeExtension
Summary: Decides whether a file extension passes the configured file type filter.
Algorithm:
  1. If fileTypes is null or empty, return true.
  2. Normalize extension to lower-case.
  3. For each type in fileTypes, if type is non-null and type.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).equals(extension), return true.
  4. Return false.
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: extension: String - file extension including the leading dot, e.g. ".java"; fileTypes: List<String> - allowed extensions including leading dot.
Output: boolean - true if no filter is configured or extension is included in fileTypes; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: extension is non-null; fileTypes is non-null or treated as empty.
Post-condition: No state change.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyfromPathSizeMb
Summary: Reads a file from a Path, enforces a maximum size, and returns its content if readable.
Algorithm:
  1. Call fileContentReader.readFileSafely(filePath, maxSizeMb).
  2. If result.readable is true, return result.content; otherwise return null.
Related Features: fileContentReader
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult
Input: filePath: Path - path to the file; maxSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes.
Output: String - file content as UTF-8 text, or null if the file is too large or unreadable.
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null; maxSizeMb > 0.
Post-condition: No state change; if readable, content is returned; otherwise null.

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb
Summary: Reads a file from a string path, enforces a maximum size, and returns its content if readable.
Algorithm:
  1. Call fileContentReader.readFileSafelyFromString(filePath, maxSizeMb).
  2. If readResult.readable is true, return readResult.content; otherwise return null.
Related Features: fileContentReader
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult
Input: filePath: String - path to the file; maxSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes.
Output: String - file content as UTF-8 text, or null if the file is too large or unreadable.
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null; maxSizeMb > 0.
Post-condition: No state change; if the file is readable, the returned string matches the file content; otherwise null is returned.

DirectoryToMarkdown::directoryToMarkdown
Summary: Convenience entry point that converts a directory to Markdown and returns the number of processed files.
Algorithm:
  1. Build options using createOptions(directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, maxFileSizeMb).
  2. Call convert(options).
  3. Return result.processedFileCount.
Related Features: createOptions(), convert()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult
Input: directoryPath: Path - root directory; outputFile: Path - target Markdown file; fileTypes: List<String> - allowed extensions; ignoreDirs: List<String> - ignored directory names; recursive: boolean - whether to scan subdirectories; maxFileSizeMb: int - maximum file size in MB.
Output: int - number of files successfully processed and added to the Markdown document.
Pre-condition: directoryPath exists and is a directory; outputFile path is writable; fileTypes and ignoreDirs are non-null.
Post-condition: The Markdown output file is written; the returned count equals the number of MarkdownFileEntry objects generated.

DirectoryToMarkdown::walkDirectory
Summary: Recursively walks a directory in stable order and writes Markdown sections for eligible files.
Algorithm:
  1. List all entries in currentDir and sort them by filename using natural lexicographic order.
  2. For each entry:
     a. Compute relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry).
     b. If directoryFilter.isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) or directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(relativePath), skip this entry entirely.
     c. If entry is a directory:
         - If optons.recursive is true, call walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result).
         - Otherwise do nothing.
     d. Else if entry is a regular file:
         - extension = getExtension(entry).
         - If shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.fileTypes) is false, skip the file.
         - Read the file with fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb).
         - If readResult.readable is false, skip the file. The readResult.skippedReason distinguishes TOO_LARGE from UNREADABLE.
         - Otherwise create markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, readResult.content).
         - Write markdownWriter.formatEntry(markdownEntry) to md, then write a blank line separator.
         - Add markdownEntry to result via result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry).
  3. Return void.
Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, walkDirectory(), getExtension(), shouldIncludeExtension(), createMarkdownFileEntry()
Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason, MarkdownWriter, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input: currentDir: Path - directory currently being visited; md: BufferedWriter - output writer; optons: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - configuration settings; baseDir: Path - root directory used to compute relative paths; result: MarkdownConversionResult - accumulator for processed files.
Output: void - the operation appends formatted Markdown to md and updates result.
Pre-condition: currentDir exists and is a directory; md is open; optons and result are non-null; baseDir is the configured root directory.
Post-condition: All eligible files under currentDir, subject to recursion and filters, have been written in deterministic order; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size().

DirectoryToMarkdown::createDefaultOptions
Summary: Creates a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions object populated with default values for optional settings.
Algorithm:
  1. Create a new DirectoryToMarkdownOptions.
  2. Set directoryPath = ""; outputFile = ""; fileTypes = empty List<String>; ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs() (node_modules, __pycache__, .git, .vscode, .idea); recursive = true; maxFileSizeMb = 10.
  3. Return the options object.
Related Features: createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input: None.
Output: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - a non-null object with default optional settings; directoryPath and outputFile are empty and must be set by the caller before conversion.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: A new options object is returned with default ignoreDirs, recursive=true, maxFileSizeMb=10, and an empty fileTypes list.

DirectoryToMarkdown::getExtension
Summary: Returns the lower-case extension of a file path, including the leading dot.
Algorithm:
  1. Get fileNamePath = path.getFileName(); if fileNamePath is null, return "".
  2. fileName = fileNamePath.toString().
  3. Find the last dot index in fileName.
  4. If no dot exists, return "".
  5. Return fileName.substring(dotIndex).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: path: Path - a file path or file name; non-null.
Output: String - extension including the leading dot, e.g. ".java"; empty string if the file name has no dot.
Pre-condition: path is non-null.
Post-condition: No state change; returned extension is lower-case.

DirectoryToMarkdown::isIgnored
Summary: Tests whether a path contains any ignored directory component.
Algorithm:
  1. If path is null or ignoreDirs is null/empty, return false.
  2. Normalize path by replacing '\' with '/'.
  3. Split normalized path on '/'.
  4. For each path component, if ignoreDirs.contains(component), return true.
  5. Return false.
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: path: String - a relative path string; may be null. ignoreDirs: List<String> - directory names to ignore; may be null or empty.
Output: boolean - true if any path component exactly matches an ignored directory name.
Pre-condition: None; null/empty inputs are accepted and return false.
Post-condition: No state change.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createMarkdownFileEntry
Summary: Builds a MarkdownFileEntry from a relative path, extension, and file content.
Algorithm:
  1. Convert relativePath.toString() to a normalized relative string by replacing '\' with '/'.
  2. If extension is null, set extension = "".
  3. Normalize extension to lower-case using Locale.ROOT.
  4. If extension is empty, set language = ""; otherwise set language = extension.substring(1) (removes the leading dot).
  5. Return new MarkdownFileEntry(content, extension, language, relativePathString).
Related Features: None
Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
Input: relativePath: Path - non-null, relative to the base directory; extension: String - file extension including the leading dot, e.g. ".java", possibly null or empty; content: String - UTF-8 text content.
Output: MarkdownFileEntry - fields content, extension, language, and relativePath are populated.
Pre-condition: relativePath and content are non-null; extension, if non-empty, starts with '.' and contains no path separators.
Post-condition: entry.extension is lower-case; entry.language equals extension without the leading dot; entry.relativePath is normalized with forward slashes.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createOptions
Summary: Converts and normalizes primitive option inputs into a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions object.
Algorithm:
  1. If fileTypes == null, set fileTypes = emptyList.
  2. If ignoreDirs == null, set ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs(); if ignoreDirs is empty, leave it empty.
  3. If maxFileSizeMb <= 0, set maxFileSizeMb = 10.
  4. Create DirectoryToMarkdownOptions with directoryPath.toString(), outputFile.toString(), fileTypes, ignoreDirs, maxFileSizeMb, and recursive.
  5. Return the options object.
Related Features: createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input: directoryPath: Path - root directory to scan; outputFile: Path - target Markdown file; fileTypes: List<String> - allowed lower-case extensions including leading dot; ignoreDirs: List<String> - ignored directory names; recursive: boolean - true to traverse subdirectories; maxFileSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes.
Output: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - non-null object carrying normalized settings.
Pre-condition: directoryPath and outputFile are non-null; directoryPath is expected to be a directory.
Post-condition: Returns an options object; null lists become empty/default lists, and non-positive maxFileSizeMb is replaced by 10.

DirectoryToMarkdown::convert
Summary: Orchestrates conversion of a configured directory into a Markdown document and returns the conversion result.
Algorithm:
  1. Validate that options is non-null.
  2. Create MarkdownConversionResult result = new MarkdownConversionResult().
  3. Create parent directories for options.outputFile if they do not exist.
  4. Open a BufferedWriter with UTF-8 encoding for options.outputFile.
  5. Call walkDirectory(Paths.get(options.directoryPath), writer, options, Paths.get(options.directoryPath), result).
  6. Close the writer in a try-with-resources block.
  7. Return result.
Related Features: walkDirectory()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult
Input: options: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - contains directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, and maxFileSizeMb.
Output: MarkdownConversionResult - contains processedFileCount and processedFiles; the output Markdown file is also written.
Pre-condition: options is non-null; options.directoryPath exists and is a readable directory; options.outputFile is writable or its parent directory can be created; fileTypes and ignoreDirs are non-null; maxFileSizeMb > 0.
Post-condition: The output file contains a Markdown section for each processed file; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size(); the file system reflects the written Markdown document.

DirectoryToMarkdown::shouldIncludeExtension
Summary: Decides whether a file extension passes the configured file type filter.
Algorithm:
  1. If fileTypes is null or empty, return true.
  2. Normalize extension to lower-case.
  3. For each type in fileTypes, if type is non-null and type.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).equals(extension), return true.
  4. Return false.
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: extension: String - file extension including the leading dot, e.g. ".java"; fileTypes: List<String> - allowed extensions including leading dot.
Output: boolean - true if no filter is configured or extension is included in fileTypes; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: extension is non-null; fileTypes is non-null or treated as empty.
Post-condition: No state change.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyfromPathSizeMb
Summary: Reads a file from a Path, enforces a maximum size, and returns its content if readable.
Algorithm:
  1. Call fileContentReader.readFileSafely(filePath, maxSizeMb).
  2. If result.readable is true, return result.content; otherwise return null.
Related Features: fileContentReader
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult
Input: filePath: Path - path to the file; maxSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes.
Output: String - file content as UTF-8 text, or null if the file is too large or unreadable.
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null; maxSizeMb > 0.
Post-condition: No state change; if readable, content is returned; otherwise null.

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb
Summary: Reads a file from a string path, enforces a maximum size, and returns its content if readable.
Algorithm:
  1. Call fileContentReader.readFileSafelyFromString(filePath, maxSizeMb).
  2. If readResult.readable is true, return readResult.content; otherwise return null.
Related Features: fileContentReader
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult
Input: filePath: String - path to the file; maxSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes.
Output: String - file content as UTF-8 text, or null if the file is too large or unreadable.
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null; maxSizeMb > 0.
Post-condition: No state change; if the file is readable, the returned string matches the file content; otherwise null is returned.

DirectoryToMarkdown::directoryToMarkdown
Summary: Convenience entry point that converts a directory to Markdown and returns the number of processed files.
Algorithm:
  1. Build options using createOptions(directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, maxFileSizeMb).
  2. Call convert(options).
  3. Return result.processedFileCount.
Related Features: createOptions(), convert()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult
Input: directoryPath: Path - root directory; outputFile: Path - target Markdown file; fileTypes: List<String> - allowed extensions; ignoreDirs: List<String> - ignored directory names; recursive: boolean - whether to scan subdirectories; maxFileSizeMb: int - maximum file size in MB.
Output: int - number of files successfully processed and added to the Markdown document.
Pre-condition: directoryPath exists and is a directory; outputFile path is writable; fileTypes and ignoreDirs are non-null.
Post-condition: The Markdown output file is written; the returned count equals the number of MarkdownFileEntry objects generated.

DirectoryToMarkdown::walkDirectory
Summary: Recursively walks a directory in stable order and writes Markdown sections for eligible files.
Algorithm:
  1. List all entries in currentDir and sort them by filename using natural lexicographic order.
  2. For each entry:
     a. Compute relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry).
     b. If directoryFilter.isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) or directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(relativePath), skip this entry entirely.
     c. If entry is a directory:
         - If optons.recursive is true, call walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result).
         - Otherwise do nothing.
     d. Else if entry is a regular file:
         - extension = getExtension(entry).
         - If shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.fileTypes) is false, skip the file.
         - Read the file with fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb).
         - If readResult.readable is false, skip the file. The readResult.skippedReason distinguishes TOO_LARGE from UNREADABLE.
         - Otherwise create markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, readResult.content).
         - Write markdownWriter.formatEntry(markdownEntry) to md, then write a blank line separator.
         - Add markdownEntry to result via result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry).
  3. Return void.
Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, walkDirectory(), getExtension(), shouldIncludeExtension(), createMarkdownFileEntry()
Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason, MarkdownWriter, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input: currentDir: Path - directory currently being visited; md: BufferedWriter - output writer; optons: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - configuration settings; baseDir: Path - root directory used to compute relative paths; result: MarkdownConversionResult - accumulator for processed files.
Output: void - the operation appends formatted Markdown to md and updates result.
Pre-condition: currentDir exists and is a directory; md is open; optons and result are non-null; baseDir is the configured root directory.
Post-condition: All eligible files under currentDir, subject to recursion and filters, have been written in deterministic order; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size().

  1. Create a new DirectoryToMarkdownOptions.
  2. Set directoryPath = ""; outputFile = ""; fileTypes = empty List<String>; ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs() (node_modules, __pycache__, .git, .vscode, .idea); recursive = true; maxFileSizeMb = 10.
  3. Return the options object.
Related Features: createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions

  3. For each entry:
     a. Compute relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry).
     b. If directoryFilter.isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) or directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(relativePath), skip this entry entirely.
     c. If entry is a directory:
         - If optons.recursive is true, call walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result).
         - Otherwise do nothing.
     d. Else if entry is a regular file:
         - extension = getExtension(entry).
         - If shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.fileTypes) is false, skip the file.
         - Read the file with fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb).
         - If readResult.readable is false, skip the file. The readResult.skippedReason distinguishes TOO_LARGE from UNREADABLE.
         - Otherwise create markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, readResult.content).
         - Write markdownWriter.formatEntry(markdownEntry) to md, then write a blank line separator.
         - Add markdownEntry to result via result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry).
  4. Return void.
Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, walkDirectory(), getExtension(), shouldIncludeExtension(), createMarkdownFileEntry()
Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason, MarkdownWriter, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input: currentDir: Path - directory currently being visited; md: BufferedWriter - output writer; optons: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - configuration settings; baseDir: Path - root directory used to compute relative paths; result: MarkdownConversionResult - accumulator for processed files.
Output: void - the operation appends formatted Markdown to md and updates result.
Pre-condition: currentDir exists and is a directory; md is open; optons and result are non-null; optons.ignoreDirs is non-null; baseDir is the configured root directory; directoryFilter is non-null.
Post-condition: All eligible files under currentDir, subject to recursion and filters, have been written in deterministic order; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size().}

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryFilter::isIgnored
Summary: Determines whether a path should be ignored by checking each path component against the configured ignored directory names.
Algorithm:
  1. If path is null or ignoreDirs is null, return false.
  2. Normalize path by replacing '\' with '/'.
  3. Split normalized path on '/'.
  4. For each path component, if ignoreDirs contains the component, return true.
  5. Return false.
Related Features: ignoreDirs
Related Classes: None
Input: path: String - a path to test, preferably relative to the scanned root, using '/' or '\' separators.
Output: boolean - true if any path component exactly matches an entry in ignoreDirs; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: ignoreDirs is initialized and non-null; path is non-null.
Post-condition: No state change; result reflects exact component matching, not substring matching.

MarkdownConversionResult::addProcessedFile
Summary: Adds a processed file entry to the result and keeps the processed file count consistent.
Algorithm:

  2. Set processedFileCount = processedFiles.size().
Related Features: processedFiles, processedFileCount
Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
Input: entry: MarkdownFileEntry - a successfully converted file record; non-null.
Output: void.
Pre-condition: result is initialized; entry is non-null.
Post-condition: processedFiles contains entry at the end; processedFileCount equals the number of entries in processedFiles.

```plantuml
    walkDirectory(currentDir : Path, md : BufferedWriter, optons : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, baseDir : Path, result : MarkdownConversionResult) : void
}
class DirectoryToMarkdownOptions {
    directoryPath : String
    fileTypes : List<String>
    ignoreDirs : List<String>
    maxFileSizeMb : int
    outputFile : String
    recursive : boolean
}
class FileContentReader {
    readFileSafely(filePath : Path, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
    readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
}
class FileReadResult {
    content : String
    readable : boolean
    skippedReason : SkipReason
}
class MarkdownConversionResult {
    processedFileCount : int
    processedFiles : List<MarkdownFileEntry>
    addProcessedFile(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : void
}
class MarkdownFileEntry {
    content : String
    extension : String
    language : String
    relativePath : String
}
class MarkdownWriter {
    formatEntry(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : String
}
enum SkipReason {
    NONE
    TOO_LARGE
    UNREADABLE
}

DirectoryToMarkdown --> MarkdownWriter : markdownWriter
MarkdownConversionResult *-- "*" MarkdownFileEntry : processedFiles
```

# Operation Specifications
MarkdownConversionResult::addProcessedFile
Summary: Adds a processed file entry to the result and keeps the processed file count consistent.
Algorithm:
  1. Append entry to processedFiles.
  2. Set processedFileCount = processedFiles.size().
Related Features: processedFiles, processedFileCount
Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
Input: entry: MarkdownFileEntry - a successfully converted file record; non-null.
Output: void.
Pre-condition: result is initialized; entry is non-null.
Post-condition: processedFiles contains entry at the end; processedFileCount equals the number of entries in processedFiles.

```java
   * Summary: Safely reads a file as UTF-8 text, enforcing a maximum size, and returns a result with a skip reason.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If filePath is null, return FileReadResult(null, false, SkipReason.UNREADABLE).
   *   2. Try:
   *        a. If Files.notExists(filePath), return FileReadResult(null, false, SkipReason.UNREADABLE).
   *        b. If Files.isRegularFile(filePath) is false, return FileReadResult(null, false, SkipReason.UNREADABLE).
   *        c. If maxSizeMb <= 0, set maxSizeMb = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_SIZE_MB (10).
   *        d. Compute maxBytes = maxSizeMb * 1024L * 1024L.
   *        e. If Files.size(filePath) > maxBytes, return FileReadResult(null, false, SkipReason.TOO_LARGE).
   *        f. Read all bytes from the file and decode them as UTF-8.
   *        g. Return FileReadResult(content, true, SkipReason.NONE).
   *      Catch IOException, SecurityException, or decoding failure:
   *        Return FileReadResult(null, false, SkipReason.UNREADABLE).
   * Related Features: None
   * Related Classes: FileReadResult, SkipReason
   * Input: filePath: Path - file to read; maxSizeMb: int - maximum file size in megabytes; if <=0, the default 10 is used.
   * Output: FileReadResult - content (String or null), readable (boolean), skippedReason (SkipReason).
   * Pre-condition: filePath is non-null; maxSizeMb is an integer value representing megabytes.
   * Post-condition: If readable is true, content contains the full UTF-8 text and skippedReason is NONE; if readable is false, content is null and skippedReason is TOO_LARGE or UNREADABLE.
```

   * @model filePathDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public FileReadResult readFileSafely(final Path filePath, final int maxSizeMb)
  {
    if (filePath == null)
    {
      FileReadResult result = JosephFactory.eINSTANCE.createFileReadResult();
      result.setContent(null);
      result.setReadable(false);
      result.setSkippedReason(SkipReason.UNREADABLE);
      return result;
    }
    try
    {
      if (Files.notExists(filePath))
      {
        FileReadResult result = JosephFactory.eINSTANCE.createFileReadResult();
        result.setContent(null);
        result.setReadable(false);
        result.setSkippedReason(SkipReason.UNREADABLE);
        return result;
      }
      if (!Files.isRegularFile(filePath))
      {
        FileReadResult result = JosephFactory.eINSTANCE.createFileReadResult();
        result.setContent(null);
        result.setReadable(false);
        result.setSkippedReason(SkipReason.UNREADABLE);
        return result;
      }
      int effectiveMaxSizeMb = maxSizeMb;
      if (effectiveMaxSizeMb <= 0)
      {
        effectiveMaxSizeMb = 10;
      }
      long maxBytes = effectiveMaxSizeMb * 1024L * 1024L;
      if (Files.size(filePath) > maxBytes)
      {
        FileReadResult result = JosephFactory.eINSTANCE.createFileReadResult();
        result.setContent(null);
        result.setReadable(false);
        result.setSkippedReason(SkipReason.TOO_LARGE);
        return result;
      }
      byte[] bytes = Files.readAllBytes(filePath);
      CharsetDecoder decoder = StandardCharsets.UTF_8.newDecoder()
        .onMalformedInput(CodingErrorAction.REPORT)
        .onUnmappableCharacter(CodingErrorAction.REPORT);
      CharBuffer charBuffer = decoder.decode(ByteBuffer.wrap(bytes));
      String content = charBuffer.toString();
      FileReadResult result = JosephFactory.eINSTANCE.createFileReadResult();
      result.setContent(content);
      result.setReadable(true);
      result.setSkippedReason(SkipReason.NONE);
      return result;
    }
    catch (IOException | SecurityException e)
    {
      FileReadResult result = JosephFactory.eINSTANCE.createFileReadResult();
      result.setContent(null);
      result.setReadable(false);
      result.setSkippedReason(SkipReason.UNREADABLE);
      return result;
    }
  }

```java
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
```
```java
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory in stable order and writes Markdown sections for eligible files.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Set directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs so that ignored-directory matching uses the configured ignore list.
   *   2. List all entries in currentDir and sort them by filename using natural lexicographic order.
   *   3. For each entry:
   *      a. Compute relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry).
   *      b. If directoryFilter.isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) or directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(relativePath), skip this entry entirely.
   *      c. If entry is a directory:
   *          - If optons.recursive is true, call walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result).
   *          - Otherwise do nothing.
   *      d. Else if entry is a regular file:
   *          - extension = getExtension(entry).
   *          - If shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.fileTypes) is false, skip the file.
   *          - Read the file with fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb).
   *          - If readResult.readable is false, skip the file. The readResult.skippedReason distinguishes TOO_LARGE from UNREADABLE.
   *          - Otherwise create markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, readResult.content).
   *          - Write markdownWriter.formatEntry(markdownEntry) to md, then write a blank line separator.
   *          - Add markdownEntry to result via result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry).
   *   4. Return void.
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, walkDirectory(), getExtension(), shouldIncludeExtension(), createMarkdownFileEntry()

   * Pre-condition: currentDir exists and is a directory; md is open; optons and result are non-null; optons.ignoreDirs is non-null; baseDir is the configured root directory; directoryFilter is non-null.
   * Post-condition: All eligible files under currentDir, subject to recursion and filters, have been written in deterministic order; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size().

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
```
```java
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory in stable order and writes Markdown sections for eligible files.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Set directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs so that ignored-directory matching uses the configured ignore list.
   *   2. List all entries in currentDir and sort them by filename using natural lexicographic order.
   *   3. For each entry:
   *      a. Compute relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry).
   *      b. If directoryFilter.isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) or directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(relativePath), skip this entry entirely.
   *      c. If entry is a directory:
   *          - If optons.recursive is true, call walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result).
   *          - Otherwise do nothing.
   *      d. Else if entry is a regular file:
   *          - extension = getExtension(entry).
   *          - If shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.fileTypes) is false, skip the file.
   *          - Read the file with fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb).
   *          - If readResult.readable is false, skip the file. The readResult.skippedReason distinguishes TOO_LARGE from UNREADABLE.
   *          - Otherwise create markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, readResult.content).
   *          - Write markdownWriter.formatEntry(markdownEntry) to md, then write a blank line separator.
   *          - Add markdownEntry to result via result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry).
   *   4. Return void.
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, walkDirectory(), getExtension(), shouldIncludeExtension(), createMarkdownFileEntry()
   * Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason, MarkdownWriter, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
   * Input: currentDir: Path - directory currently being visited; md: BufferedWriter - output writer; optons: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - configuration settings; baseDir: Path - root directory used to compute relative paths; result: MarkdownConversionResult - accumulator for processed files.
   * Output: void - the operation appends formatted Markdown to md and updates result.
   * Pre-condition: currentDir exists and is a directory; md is open; optons and result are non-null; optons.ignoreDirs is non-null; baseDir is the configured root directory; directoryFilter is non-null.
   * Post-condition: All eligible files under currentDir, subject to recursion and filters, have been written in deterministic order; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size().
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model currentDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path" mdDataType="edu.joseph.BufferedWriter" baseDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
```
