  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory in stable order and writes Markdown sections for eligible files.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Set directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs so that ignored-directory matching uses the configured ignore list.
   *   2. List all entries in currentDir and sort them by filename using natural lexicographic order.
   *   3. For each entry:
   *      a. Compute relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry).
   *      b. If directoryFilter.isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) or directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(relativePath), skip this entry entirely.
   *      c. If entry is a directory:
   *          - If optons.recursive is true, call walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result).
   *          - Otherwise do nothing.
   *      d. Else if entry is a regular file:
   *          - extension = getExtension(entry).
   *          - If shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.fileTypes) is false, skip the file.
   *          - Read the file with fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb).
   *          - If readResult.readable is false, skip the file. The readResult.skippedReason distinguishes TOO_LARGE from UNREADABLE.
   *          - Otherwise create markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, readResult.content).
   *          - Write markdownWriter.formatEntry(markdownEntry) to md, then write a blank line separator.
   *          - Add markdownEntry to result via result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry).
   *   4. Return void.
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, walkDirectory(), getExtension(), shouldIncludeExtension(), createMarkdownFileEntry()
   * Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason, MarkdownWriter, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
   * Input: currentDir: Path - directory currently being visited; md: BufferedWriter - output writer; optons: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - configuration settings; baseDir: Path - root directory used to compute relative paths; result: MarkdownConversionResult - accumulator for processed files.
   * Output: void - the operation appends formatted Markdown to md and updates result.
   * Pre-condition: currentDir exists and is a directory; md is open; optons and result are non-null; optons.ignoreDirs is non-null; baseDir is the configured root directory; directoryFilter is non-null.
   * Post-condition: All eligible files under currentDir, subject to recursion and filters, have been written in deterministic order; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size().
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model currentDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path" mdDataType="edu.joseph.BufferedWriter" baseDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path"

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory in stable order and writes Markdown sections for eligible files.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Set directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs so that ignored-directory matching uses the configured ignore list.
   *   2. List all entries in currentDir and sort them by filename using natural lexicographic order.
   *   3. For each entry:
   *      a. Compute relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry).
   *      b. If directoryFilter.isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) or directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(relativePath), skip this entry entirely.
   *      c. If entry is a directory:
   *          - If optons.recursive is true, call walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result).
   *          - Otherwise do nothing.
   *      d. Else if entry is a regular file:
   *          - extension = getExtension(entry).
   *          - If shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.fileTypes) is false, skip the file.
   *          - Read the file with fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb).
   *          - If readResult.readable is false, skip the file. The readResult.skippedReason distinguishes TOO_LARGE from UNREADABLE.
   *          - Otherwise create markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, readResult.content).
   *          - Write markdownWriter.formatEntry(markdownEntry) to md, then write a blank line separator.
   *          - Add markdownEntry to result via result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry).
   *   4. Return void.
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, walkDirectory(), getExtension(), shouldIncludeExtension(), createMarkdownFileEntry()
   * Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason, MarkdownWriter, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
   * Input: currentDir: Path - directory currently being visited; md: BufferedWriter - output writer; optons: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - configuration settings; baseDir: Path - root directory used to compute relative paths; result: MarkdownConversionResult - accumulator for processed files.
   * Output: void - the operation appends formatted Markdown to md and updates result.
   * Pre-condition: currentDir exists and is a directory; md is open; optons and result are non-null; optons.ignoreDirs is non-null; baseDir is the configured root directory; directoryFilter is non-null.
   * Post-condition: All eligible files under currentDir, subject to recursion and filters, have been written in deterministic order; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size().
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model currentDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path" mdDataType="edu.joseph.BufferedWriter" baseDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Decides whether a file extension passes the configured file type filter.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If fileTypes is null or empty, return true.
   *   2. Normalize extension to lower-case.
   *   3. For each type in fileTypes, if type is non-null and type.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).equals(extension), return true.
   *   4. Return false.

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory in stable order and writes Markdown sections for eligible files.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Set directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs so that ignored-directory matching uses the configured ignore list.
   *   2. List all entries in currentDir and sort them by filename using natural lexicographic order.
   *   3. For each entry:
   *      a. Compute relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry).
   *      b. If directoryFilter.isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) or directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(relativePath), skip this entry entirely.
   *      c. If entry is a directory:
   *          - If optons.recursive is true, call walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result).
   *          - Otherwise do nothing.
   *      d. Else if entry is a regular file:
   *          - extension = getExtension(entry).
   *          - If shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.fileTypes) is false, skip the file.
   *          - Read the file with fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb).
   *          - If readResult.readable is false, skip the file. The readResult.skippedReason distinguishes TOO_LARGE from UNREADABLE.
   *          - Otherwise create markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, readResult.content).
   *          - Write markdownWriter.formatEntry(markdownEntry) to md, then write a blank line separator.
   *          - Add markdownEntry to result via result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry).
   *   4. Return void.
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, walkDirectory(), getExtension(), shouldIncludeExtension(), createMarkdownFileEntry()
   * Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason, MarkdownWriter, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
   * Input: currentDir: Path - directory currently being visited; md: BufferedWriter - output writer; optons: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - configuration settings; baseDir: Path - root directory used to compute relative paths; result: MarkdownConversionResult - accumulator for processed files.
   * Output: void - the operation appends formatted Markdown to md and updates result.
   * Pre-condition: currentDir exists and is a directory; md is open; optons and result are non-null; optons.ignoreDirs is non-null; baseDir is the configured root directory; directoryFilter is non-null.
   * Post-condition: All eligible files under currentDir, subject to recursion and filters, have been written in deterministic order; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size().
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model currentDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path" mdDataType="edu.joseph.BufferedWriter" baseDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory in stable order and writes Markdown sections for eligible files.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Set directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs so that ignored-directory matching uses the configured ignore list.
   *   2. List all entries in currentDir and sort them by filename using natural lexicographic order.
   *   3. For each entry:
   *      a. Compute relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry).
   *      b. If directoryFilter.isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) or directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(relativePath), skip this entry entirely.
   *      c. If entry is a directory:
   *          - If optons.recursive is true, call walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result).
   *          - Otherwise do nothing.
   *      d. Else if entry is a regular file:
   *          - extension = getExtension(entry).
   *          - If shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.fileTypes) is false, skip the file.
   *          - Read the file with fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb).
   *          - If readResult.readable is false, skip the file. The readResult.skippedReason distinguishes TOO_LARGE from UNREADABLE.
   *          - Otherwise create markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, readResult.content).
   *          - Write markdownWriter.formatEntry(markdownEntry) to md, then write a blank line separator.
   *          - Add markdownEntry to result via result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry).
   *   4. Return void.
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, walkDirectory(), getExtension(), shouldIncludeExtension(), createMarkdownFileEntry()
   * Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason, MarkdownWriter, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
   * Input: currentDir: Path - directory currently being visited; md: BufferedWriter - output writer; optons: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - configuration settings; baseDir: Path - root directory used to compute relative paths; result: MarkdownConversionResult - accumulator for processed files.
   * Output: void - the operation appends formatted Markdown to md and updates result.
   * Pre-condition: currentDir exists and is a directory; md is open; optons and result are non-null; optons.ignoreDirs is non-null; baseDir is the configured root directory; directoryFilter is non-null.
   * Post-condition: All eligible files under currentDir, subject to recursion and filters, have been written in deterministic order; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size().
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model currentDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path" mdDataType="edu.joseph.BufferedWriter" baseDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory in stable order and writes Markdown sections for eligible files.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Set directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs so that ignored-directory matching uses the configured ignore list.
   *   2. List all entries in currentDir and sort them by filename using natural lexicographic order.
   *   3. For each entry:
   *      a. Compute relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry).
   *      b. If directoryFilter.isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) or directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(relativePath), skip this entry entirely.
   *      c. If entry is a directory:
   *          - If optons.recursive is true, call walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result).
   *          - Otherwise do nothing.
   *      d. Else if entry is a regular file:
   *          - extension = getExtension(entry).
   *          - If shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.fileTypes) is false, skip the file.
   *          - Read the file with fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb).
   *          - If readResult.readable is false, skip the file. The readResult.skippedReason distinguishes TOO_LARGE from UNREADABLE.
   *          - Otherwise create markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, readResult.content).
   *          - Write markdownWriter.formatEntry(markdownEntry) to md, then write a blank line separator.
   *          - Add markdownEntry to result via result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry).
   *   4. Return void.
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, walkDirectory(), getExtension(), shouldIncludeExtension(), createMarkdownFileEntry()
   * Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason, MarkdownWriter, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
   * Input: currentDir: Path - directory currently being visited; md: BufferedWriter - output writer; optons: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - configuration settings; baseDir: Path - root directory used to compute relative paths; result: MarkdownConversionResult - accumulator for processed files.
   * Output: void - the operation appends formatted Markdown to md and updates result.
   * Pre-condition: currentDir exists and is a directory; md is open; optons and result are non-null; optons.ignoreDirs is non-null; baseDir is the configured root directory; directoryFilter is non-null.
   * Post-condition: All eligible files under currentDir, subject to recursion and filters, have been written in deterministic order; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size().
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model currentDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path" mdDataType="edu.joseph.BufferedWriter" baseDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated

  public MarkdownConversionResult convert(final DirectoryToMarkdownOptions options)
  {
    if (options == null)
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("options must not be null");
    }

    MarkdownConversionResult result = JosephFactory.eINSTANCE.createMarkdownConversionResult();

    Path outputPath = Paths.get(options.getOutputFile());
    Path outputParent = outputPath.getParent();
    if (outputParent != null)
    {
      try
      {
        Files.createDirectories(outputParent);
      }
      catch (IOException e)
      {
        throw new RuntimeException("Failed to create parent directories for output file: " + outputPath, e);
      }
    }

    Path root = Paths.get(options.getDirectoryPath());
    try (BufferedWriter writer = Files.newBufferedWriter(outputPath, StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
    {
      walkDirectory(root, writer, options, root, result);
    }
    catch (IOException e)
    {
      throw new RuntimeException("Failed to write Markdown output file: " + outputPath, e);
    }

    return result;
  }

   * Pre-condition: directoryPath and outputFile are non-null; directoryPath is expected to be a directory.
   * Post-condition: Returns an options object; null lists become empty/default lists, and non-positive maxFileSizeMb is replaced by 10.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model directoryPathDataType="edu.joseph.Path" outputFileDataType="edu.joseph.Path" fileTypesMany="true" ignoreDirsMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public DirectoryToMarkdownOptions createOptions(final Path directoryPath, final Path outputFile, final EList<String> fileTypes, final EList<String> ignoreDirs, final boolean recursive, final int maxFileSizeMb)
  {
    DirectoryToMarkdownOptions options = JosephFactory.eINSTANCE.createDirectoryToMarkdownOptions();
    options.setDirectoryPath(directoryPath.toString());
    options.setOutputFile(outputFile.toString());

    if (fileTypes == null)
    {
      options.getFileTypes().clear();
    }
    else
    {
      options.getFileTypes().addAll(fileTypes);
    }

    if (ignoreDirs == null)
    {
      options.getIgnoreDirs().addAll(createDefaultIgnoreDirs());
    }
    else
    {
      options.getIgnoreDirs().addAll(ignoreDirs);
    }

    options.setRecursive(recursive);
    options.setMaxFileSizeMb(maxFileSizeMb <= 0 ? 10 : maxFileSizeMb);

    return options;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory in stable order and writes Markdown sections for eligible files.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Set directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs so that ignored-directory matching uses the configured ignore list.
   *   2. List all entries in currentDir and sort them by filename using natural lexicographic order.
   *   3. For each entry:
   *      a. Compute relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry).
   *      b. If directoryFilter.isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) or directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(relativePath), skip this entry entirely.
   *      c. If entry is a directory:
   *          - If optons.recursive is true, call walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result).
   *          - Otherwise do nothing.
   *      d. Else if entry is a regular file:
   *          - extension = getExtension(entry).
   *          - If shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.fileTypes) is false, skip the file.
   *          - Read the file with fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb).
   *          - If readResult.readable is false, skip the file. The readResult.skippedReason distinguishes TOO_LARGE from UNREADABLE.
   *          - Otherwise create markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, readResult.content).
   *          - Write markdownWriter.formatEntry(markdownEntry) to md, then write a blank line separator.
   *          - Add markdownEntry to result via result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry).
   *   4. Return void.
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, walkDirectory(), getExtension(), shouldIncludeExtension(), createMarkdownFileEntry()
   * Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason, MarkdownWriter, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
   * Input: currentDir: Path - directory currently being visited; md: BufferedWriter - output writer; optons: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions - configuration settings; baseDir: Path - root directory used to compute relative paths; result: MarkdownConversionResult - accumulator for processed files.
   * Output: void - the operation appends formatted Markdown to md and updates result.
   * Pre-condition: currentDir exists and is a directory; md is open; optons and result are non-null; optons.ignoreDirs is non-null; baseDir is the configured root directory; directoryFilter is non-null.
   * Post-condition: All eligible files under currentDir, subject to recursion and filters, have been written in deterministic order; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size().
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model currentDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path" mdDataType="edu.joseph.BufferedWriter" baseDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public void walkDirectory(final Path currentDir, final BufferedWriter md, final DirectoryToMarkdownOptions optons, final Path baseDir, final MarkdownConversionResult result)
  {
    getDirectoryFilter().getIgnoreDirs().clear();
    getDirectoryFilter().getIgnoreDirs().addAll(optons.getIgnoreDirs());

    try
    {
      try (Stream<Path> stream = Files.list(currentDir))
      {
        List<Path> entries = stream
            .sorted(Comparator.comparing(p -> p.getFileName().toString()))
            .collect(Collectors.toList());

        for (Path entry : entries)
        {
          Path relativePath = baseDir.relativize(entry);

          if (getDirectoryFilter().isIgnored(relativePath.toString()) || getDirectoryFilter().isIgnoredPath(relativePath))
          {
            continue;
          }

          if (Files.isDirectory(entry))
          {
            if (optons.isRecursive())
            {
              walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result);
            }
          }
          else if (Files.isRegularFile(entry))
          {
            String extension = getExtension(entry);
            if (!shouldIncludeExtension(extension, optons.getFileTypes()))
            {
              continue;
            }

            FileReadResult readResult = getFileContentReader().readFileSafely(entry, optons.getMaxFileSizeMb());
            if (!readResult.isReadable())
            {
              continue;
            }

            MarkdownFileEntry markdownEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath, extension, readResult.getContent());
            md.write(getMarkdownWriter().formatEntry(markdownEntry));
            md.newLine();
            result.addProcessedFile(markdownEntry);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    catch (IOException e)
    {
      throw new RuntimeException("Failed to walk directory: " + currentDir, e);
    }
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Builds a MarkdownFileEntry from a relative path, extension, and file content.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Convert relativePath.toString() to a normalized relative string by replacing '\' with '/'.
   *   2. If extension is null, set extension = "".
   *   3. Normalize extension to lower-case using Locale.ROOT.
   *   4. If extension is empty, set language = ""; otherwise set language = extension.substring(1) (removes the leading dot).
   *   5. Return new MarkdownFileEntry(content, extension, language, relativePathString).
   * Related Features: None
   * Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
   * Input: relativePath: Path - non-null, relative to the base directory; extension: String - file extension including the leading dot, e.g. ".java", possibly null or empty; content: String - UTF-8 text content.
   * Output: MarkdownFileEntry - fields content, extension, language, and relativePath are populated.
   * Pre-condition: relativePath and content are non-null; extension, if non-empty, starts with '.' and contains no path separators.
   * Post-condition: entry.extension is lower-case; entry.language equals extension without the leading dot; entry.relativePath is normalized with forward slashes.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model relativePathDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public MarkdownFileEntry createMarkdownFileEntry(final Path relativePath, final String extension, final String content)
  {
    String relativePathString = relativePath.toString().replace('\\', '/');

    String ext = extension;
    if (ext == null)
    {
      ext = "";
    }
    ext = ext.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);

    String language = "";
    if (!ext.isEmpty())
    {
      language = ext.substring(1);
    }

    MarkdownFileEntry entry = JosephFactory.eINSTANCE.createMarkdownFileEntry();
    entry.setContent(content);
    entry.setExtension(ext);
    entry.setLanguage(language);
    entry.setRelativePath(relativePathString);

    return entry;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Decides whether a file extension passes the configured file type filter.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If fileTypes is null or empty, return true.
   *   2. Normalize extension to lower-case.
   *   3. For each type in fileTypes, if type is non-null and type.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).equals(extension), return true.
   *   4. Return false.
   * Related Features: None
   * Related Classes: None
   * Input: extension: String - file extension including the leading dot, e.g. ".java"; fileTypes: List<String> - allowed extensions including leading dot.
   * Output: boolean - true if no filter is configured or extension is included in fileTypes; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: extension is non-null; fileTypes is non-null or treated as empty.
   * Post-condition: No state change.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model fileTypesMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean shouldIncludeExtension(final String extension, final EList<String> fileTypes)
  {
    if (fileTypes == null || fileTypes.isEmpty())
    {
      return true;
    }

    String normalizedExtension = extension.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
    for (String type : fileTypes)
    {
      if (type != null && type.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).equals(normalizedExtension))
      {
        return true;
      }
    }

    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Returns the lower-case extension of a file path, including the leading dot.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Get fileNamePath = path.getFileName(); if fileNamePath is null, return "".
   *   2. fileName = fileNamePath.toString().
   *   3. Find the last dot index in fileName.
   *   4. If no dot exists, return "".
   *   5. Return fileName.substring(dotIndex).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).
   * Related Features: None
   * Related Classes: None
   * Input: path: Path - a file path or file name; non-null.
   * Output: String - extension including the leading dot, e.g. ".java"; empty string if the file name has no dot.
   * Pre-condition: path is non-null.
   * Post-condition: No state change; returned extension is lower-case.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model pathDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public String getExtension(final Path path)
  {
    Path fileNamePath = path.getFileName();
    if (fileNamePath == null)
    {
      return "";
    }

    String fileName = fileNamePath.toString();
    int dotIndex = fileName.lastIndexOf('.');
    if (dotIndex < 0)
    {
      return "";
    }

    return fileName.substring(dotIndex).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
  }

import java.util.List;

/**
 * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
 * A representation of the model object '<em><b>Directory To Markdown</b></em>'.

You will be given a **Java Class** that is originally generated by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true` and later is completed by LLMs. Your task is to fix the **Compilation Errors** by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. Read the error messages carefully.
3. Do your best to fix the errors locally without introducing new types, fields, and methods.
4. You should not remove faulty methods to eliminate compilation errors.
5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.
